Result: 将SGA集成到多个预测骨干网络中，在9个真实数据集（涵盖电力、金融、天气、医疗监测、人类活动、气候记录等）上与标准自注意力和轻量注意力变体对比，SGA在保持与最先进注意力机制相竞争的预测性能的同时，显著提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: SGA作为一种高效的注意力替代方案，在时间序列预测任务中实现了线性复杂度，兼顾了推理效率与预测性能，为部署导向的时间序列预测提供了有力证据。

Original Abstract: Transformer architectures have shown strong potential in time series forecasting, where multi-head self-attention is widely used to capture temporal dependencies across historical timestamps. However, standard self-attention has quadratic time and memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. This cost may limit its use in resource-constrained or high-throughput forecasting systems, where fast and memory-efficient inference is important. Through qualitative and quantitative analyses, we observe that self-attention maps in time series forecasting often contain redundant patterns across different timestamps. This phenomenon can be related to the repeated temporal patterns and relatively stable temporal correlations in many real-world time series. Motivated by this observation, we propose Self-Gating Attention (SGA), a plug-and-play attention mechanism that represents the attention score with a shared learnable matrix and an input-dependent residual component. The shared matrix captures common attention patterns, while the residual component captures input-dependent variations. In this way, SGA avoids the query and key projections used in standard attention score computation, leading to linear time and score-matrix memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. We integrate SGA into several forecasting backbones and compare it with standard self-attention and lightweight attention variants on nine publicly available real-world datasets covering electricity, finance, weather, medical monitoring, human activity, and climate records. The results show that SGA improves inference efficiency on public benchmarks while maintaining competitive forecasting performance against state-of-the-art attention mechanisms. These benchmark results provide deployment-oriented evidence.

中文摘要翻译: Transformer架构在时间序列预测中展现出强大潜力，其中多头自注意力被广泛用于捕获历史时间戳之间的时间依赖关系。然而，标准自注意力的时间和内存复杂度相对于回看长度呈二次增长。这一代价可能限制其在资源受限或高吞吐量预测系统中的使用，而这类系统对快速且内存高效的推理有重要需求。通过定性和定量分析，我们观察到时间序列预测中的自注意力图在不同时间步上常包含冗余模式。这一现象与许多真实世界时间序列中重复出现的时间模式以及相对稳定的时间相关性有关。受此观察启发，我们提出了自门控注意力（Self-Gating Attention, SGA），一种即插即用的注意力机制，它将注意力分数表示为一个共享可学习矩阵与一个输入依赖残差分量的组合。共享矩阵捕获通用的注意力模式，而残差分量捕获输入依赖的变化。通过这种方式，SGA避免了标准注意力分数计算中使用的查询和键投影，从而在回看长度上实现了线性的时间和分数矩阵内存复杂度。我们将SGA集成到多个预测骨干网络中，并在涵盖电力、金融、天气、医疗监测、人类活动及气候记录的九个公开真实世界数据集上，将其与标准自注意力及轻量级注意力变体进行比较。结果表明，SGA在公共基准上提升了推理效率，同时保持了与最先进注意力机制相竞争的预测性能。这些基准测试结果为部署导向的决策提供了有力依据。

</details>


### [237] [DecompRL: Solving Harder Problems by Learning Modular Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02390)
*Juliette Decugis,Fabian Gloeckle,Francis Bach,Taco Cohen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DecompRL，一种通过将问题分解为可复用子模块并强化学习训练模块化代码生成的方法，在代码生成任务中以约50倍的GPU成本降低，解决了标准方法因搜索空间过大而无法触及的难题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法（重复采样和强化学习）在基础策略生成正确解的概率接近零时均会失效，因为无论多少采样或梯度信号都无法克服过大的搜索空间。作者希望改变思路，通过分解问题来降低任务难度，而非提高采样强度。

Method: 提出DecompRL，一种显式学习分解和实现层级代码结构的强化学习算法。该算法训练模型将问题分解为更小、可独立求解的子函数，并重新组合这些实现。通过组合n个模块的各k种实现，可产生k^n个候选解，从而将瓶颈从GPU推理转移到廉价的CPU评估。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上（Qwen 2.5 7B和Code World Model 32B），DecompRL在每题超过10^5个token的情况下优于标准和多样性优化的强化学习基线，解决了标准生成无法触及的问题，并将GPU token成本降低约50倍。

Conclusion: 通过分解-重组策略，DecompRL有效克服了基础策略搜索空间过大的瓶颈，显著提升了大语言模型在复杂代码生成问题上的求解能力，同时大幅降低了计算成本。

Original Abstract: How can Large Language Models (LLMs) solve problems they currently cannot? Repeated sampling scales test-time compute but GPU cost grows linearly with attempts, while reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards improves single-attempt accuracy at the expense of sample diversity. Both strategies ultimately fail when the base policy has near-zero probability of producing a correct solution: no amount of sampling or gradient signal can overcome a search space that is simply too large. We take a different approach: rather than sampling harder, we make the task easier by decomposing problems into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions whose implementations can be recombined. Since off-the-shelf models are not trained for this modular generation, we introduce DecompRL, an RL algorithm that explicitly learns to decompose and implement hierarchical code structures. Recombining $k$ implementations of $n$ modules yields up to $k^{n}$ candidate solutions, shifting the bottleneck from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation and cutting GPU token cost by $\sim$50$\times$. On LiveCodeBench and CodeContests (Qwen~2.5~7B, Code World Model~32B), DecompRL outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond $10^5$ tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot reach.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）如何解决当前无法解决的问题？重复采样增加了测试时的计算量，但GPU成本随尝试次数线性增长，而基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RL）虽能提高单次尝试的准确率，却以牺牲样本多样性为代价。当基础策略生成正确解的概率趋近于零时，这两种策略最终都会失效：无论多少采样或梯度信号都无法克服过大的搜索空间。我们采用了不同的方法：与其加大采样强度，不如通过将问题分解为更小、可独立求解的子函数来降低任务难度，这些子函数的实现可以重新组合。由于现成模型并未针对这种模块化生成进行训练，我们引入了DecompRL，这是一种显式学习如何分解和实现层级代码结构的强化学习算法。将n个模块的各k种实现进行重组，可产生多达k^n个候选解，从而将瓶颈从GPU推理转移到廉价的CPU评估上，并将GPU token成本降低约50倍。在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上（Qwen 2.5 7B，Code World Model 32B），DecompRL在每题超过10^5个token的情况下优于标准和多样性优化的强化学习基线，解决了标准生成无法触及的问题。

</details>


### [238] [Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02423)
*Zhuowei Chen,Liwei Chen,Christian Schunn,Raquel Coelho,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuFS提出了一种基于神经元激活模式的主动少样本学习框架，通过模型内部动态而非输出级信号来选择最有价值的样本进行标注，在推理和文本分类任务上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的主动少样本学习方法主要依赖输出级信号（如预测熵或基于外部嵌入的语义相似度）来选择样本，忽略了模型内部动态信息（如神经元激活模式），而这些内部信息能够更精准地定位模型的知识盲区。

Method: NeuFS框架包含两个核心部分：(1) 使用神经元激活模式直接表示样本；(2) 双标准选择策略：通过神经元模式确保少样本多样性以覆盖更广的示例范围，同时通过量化神经元一致性来识别LLM容易产生幻觉的信息性和挑战性样本。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuFS在推理和文本分类任务中均优于现有AFSL基线方法。消融研究进一步证明，内部神经元激活比外部嵌入提供了更本质和有效的选择信号。

Conclusion: NeuFS通过利用模型内部神经元激活模式进行样本选择，验证了从输出级代理转向模型内部动态这一范式的优越性，为主动少样本学习提供了更有效的思路。

Original Abstract: Active Few-Shot Learning (AFSL) adapts LLMs to specialized domains by identifying the most valuable unlabeled samples for annotation and use as few-shot demonstrations, effectively reducing human annotation costs while promoting high performance. However, existing methods typically rely on output-level signals for sample identification, such as predictive entropy or semantic similarities with test-time data based on external embeddings, which often overlook models' internal dynamics, which could pinpoint specific knowledge gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuFS, a Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning framework that shifts the selection paradigm from output-level proxies to models' internal dynamics. NeuFS utilizes neuron activation patterns to represent sample directly, and includes a dual-criteria selection strategy that: (1) ensures few-shot sample diversity with neuron patterns for broader example coverage, while (2) prioritizing on identifying informative and challenging few-shot samples LLMs tend to hallucinate by quantifying neuron consensus. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that NeuFS excels in both reasoning and text classification tasks, outperforming existing AFSL baselines. Ablation studies further highlight that internal neuron activations provide a more principled and effective selection signal than external embeddings, validating the superiority of the proposed NeuFS.

中文摘要翻译: 主动少样本学习通过识别最有价值的未标注样本进行标注并作为少样本示例，使大语言模型适应专业领域，从而在降低人工标注成本的同时提升性能。然而，现有方法通常依赖输出级信号（如预测熵或基于外部嵌入的与测试数据的语义相似度）来识别样本，这些方法常常忽略了能够精确定位特定知识盲区的模型内部动态。为弥补这一不足，我们提出了NeuFS——一种基于神经元感知的主动少样本学习框架，该框架将样本选择范式从输出级代理转向模型内部动态。NeuFS利用神经元激活模式直接表示样本，并包含一个双标准选择策略：(1) 通过神经元模式确保少样本多样性以覆盖更广的示例范围；(2) 通过量化神经元一致性来优先识别大语言模型容易产生幻觉的信息性和挑战性样本。在三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuFS在推理和文本分类任务中均表现出色，优于现有的主动少样本学习基线方法。消融研究进一步凸显了内部神经元激活相比外部嵌入能够提供更本质和有效的选择信号，验证了所提出的NeuFS的优越性。

</details>


### [239] [QFedAgent: Quantum-Enhanced Personalized Federated Learning for Multi-Agent Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02426)
*Quoc Bao Phan,Tuy Tan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架QFedAgent，用于多智能体活动识别。通过变分量子电路融合模块替代经典融合模块，在保持97.7%准确率的同时将参数从33K降至72个，实现约10倍参数缩减。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多智能体系统产生的异构、非独立同分布的多模态传感器数据会降低传统联邦学习算法性能，而经典融合模块引入大量参数开销和通信成本，亟需一种参数高效且能处理非IID多模态数据的联邦学习框架。

Method: 提出QFedAgent混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架，采用变分量子电路（VQC）作为融合模块，通过量子态编码和纠缠机制建模加速度计-陀螺仪之间的交互，仅需72个量子旋转参数即可完成多模态融合。

Result: 在OPPORTUNITY数据集上，基于主体非IID划分的实验达到97.7%的平均测试准确率；VQC融合模块仅需72个量子旋转参数，而经典MLP融合需要33K参数，实现约10倍总参数量缩减。

Conclusion: 参数高效的量子融合在与传统联邦基线相比时仍具有竞争力，验证了量子-经典混合范式在联邦学习多模态融合中的可行性和参数效率优势。

Original Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 联邦学习（FL）能够在分布式设备间进行协作模型训练而无需共享原始数据，因此适用于隐私敏感的机器人传感应用。然而，多智能体系统生成的异构且非独立同分布（non-IID）的多模态传感器流会降低传统联邦学习算法的性能，同时经典融合模块会引入大量参数开销和通信成本。本文提出QFedAgent，一种面向多智能体活动识别的混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架。该方法集成了一种变分量子电路融合模块，通过量子态编码和纠缠机制对加速度计-陀螺仪之间的交互进行建模，仅需72个量子旋转参数，而基于经典多层感知机的融合需要33K参数，实现了约10倍的总参数量缩减。在OPPORTUNITY数据集上，基于主体非独立同分布划分的实验达到了97.7%的平均测试准确率，证实了参数高效的量子融合在与传统联邦基线相比时仍具有竞争力。

</details>


### [240] [Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02437)
*Sanjeev Shrestha,Hui Liu,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Exformer（极端自适应Transformer），通过极端自适应注意力机制（包含Local、Stride和Extreme三个稀疏组件）显式建模正常事件与极端事件之间的时序依赖关系，在四个真实水文径流数据集上实现了更优的3天预测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 时间序列中罕见但关键的极端事件常被现有Transformer模型均匀对待而未能充分表征，尤其在具有高度偏斜分布的水文径流预测中，极端峰值对洪水监测、水资源管理和预警系统至关重要。

Method: 提出Exformer框架，核心是极端自适应注意力机制，包含三个稀疏注意力组件：Local（捕获短期局部依赖）、Stride（捕获周期依赖）和Extreme（选择性建模正常与极端径流模式之间的事件感知依赖）。

Result: 在四个真实水文径流数据集上，Exformer在3天预测任务中优于多个现有最先进基线模型。

Conclusion: 显式引入极端感知注意力机制能够有效提升Transformer模型在不平衡时间序列（包含罕见但重要事件）上的预测能力。

Original Abstract: Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.

中文摘要翻译: 当底层数据包含罕见但关键的极端事件时，时间序列预测仍然具有挑战性。这一问题在水文预测中尤为重要，因为径流分布通常高度偏斜，极端峰值对洪水监测、水资源管理和预警系统具有重大影响。尽管基于Transformer的预测模型通过建模长程时序依赖取得了优异性能，但它们通常均匀对待所有时间点，因此可能对罕见的极端模式表征不足。本文提出Exformer（极端自适应Transformer），一种旨在显式建模涉及正常与极端事件的时序依赖的预测框架。Exformer引入了由三个稀疏组件组成的极端自适应注意力机制：Local（局部）、Stride（步进）和Extreme（极端）。Local和Stride组件分别捕获短期和周期性的时序依赖，而Extreme组件选择性建模正常与极端径流模式之间的事件感知依赖。在四个真实世界水文径流数据集上的实验表明，Exformer在3天预测任务中优于现有最先进基线。我们的研究结果表明，显式引入极端感知注意力能够提升Transformer模型在不平衡且包含罕见但重要事件的时间序列上的预测能力。

</details>


### [241] [Understanding the Robustness of Distributed Self-Supervised Learning Frameworks Against Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02447)
*Xuanyu Chen,Nan Yang,Shuai Wang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）在非独立同分布数据下的鲁棒性进行了严格的理论分析，发现掩码图像建模（MIM）比对比学习（CL）对异构数据更鲁棒，且联邦学习（FL）的鲁棒性不低于去中心化学习（DecL）。同时提出了MAR损失函数作为理论的实际应用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）面临数据异构性的关键挑战，但现有研究缺乏对不同D-SSL框架在该挑战下表现的理论理解。作者旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 1) 对D-SSL框架在非IID设置下的鲁棒性进行严格理论分析；2) 提出MAR损失函数（MIM目标与局部到全局对齐正则化的改进）。

Result: 理论结果表明，MIM预训练比对比学习对异构数据更鲁棒；D-SSL的鲁棒性随平均网络连通性增加而提高，即FL的鲁棒性不低于DecL。实验验证了理论发现并证实了MAR损失的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来D-SSL算法设计提供了坚实的理论基础，并通过MAR损失展示了理论的实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 最近的研究引入了分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）方法，以利用大量未标记的去中心化数据。然而，D-SSL面临着数据异构性的关键挑战，并且关于不同D-SSL框架如何应对这一挑战的理论理解十分有限。为填补这一空白，我们对非独立同分布（non-IID）设置下D-SSL框架的鲁棒性进行了严格的理论分析。结果表明，使用掩码图像建模（MIM）进行预训练本质上比对比学习（CL）对异构数据更具鲁棒性，并且去中心化SSL的鲁棒性随着平均网络连通性的增加而提高，这意味着联邦学习（FL）的鲁棒性不低于去中心化学习（DecL）。这些发现为未来D-SSL算法的设计提供了坚实的理论基础。为进一步说明我们理论的实际意义，我们引入了MAR损失，即对MIM目标进行局部到全局对齐正则化的改进。跨模型架构和分布式设置的大量实验验证了我们的理论见解，并进一步证实了MAR损失作为我们分析的一个应用的有效性。

</details>


### [242] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02460)
*Zhuowei Chen,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经元在线自蒸馏（Neuron-OPSD）框架，利用内部神经元激活指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，通过在线策略蒸馏实现无需标注的自蒸馏，在提升领域内任务性能的同时保持跨领域泛化并缓解校准误差膨胀。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无标注自进化方法（SFT/GRPO变体、基于奖励的在线策略RL）存在领域外性能下降和校准误差膨胀的问题，亟需一种既能提升领域内任务性能又不牺牲泛化能力和校准质量的标注-free框架。

Method: 提出Neuron-OPSD框架，核心思想是利用模型内部神经元激活（neuron activations）来指导两个关键环节：（1）训练数据选择；（2）教师上下文（teacher context）构建。然后通过在线策略蒸馏（on-policy distillation）从教师分布中训练模型，全程无需真实标签。

Result: 在专业领域基准测试上，Neuron-OPSD相比现有无标注基线方法，提升了领域内任务性能，同时保持了跨领域泛化能力，并缓解了校准误差膨胀问题。

Conclusion: Neuron-OPSD框架适用于在线交互或外部监督成本高昂或不可行的场景，与依赖已标注奖励轨迹的离线RL方法在概念上截然不同，为无需真实交互反馈或人工标注的大模型后训练提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.

中文摘要翻译: 在缺乏真实世界交互反馈或人工标注监督的情况下对大型语言模型（LLM）进行后训练仍然具有挑战性，尤其是在专家标注获取成本高昂的专业领域。最近的无标注自进化方法通过使用模型自身的输出作为监督信号来应对这一问题：它们通过额外上下文构建一个教师模型，并通过多数投票聚合多次rollout的预测结果以生成伪标签。然而，这些方法并非没有缺陷：基于SFT和GRPO的变体会出现领域外性能下降，而基于奖励的在线策略RL则会膨胀校准误差。在本文中，我们提出了神经元在线自蒸馏（Neuron-OPSD），一个用于无标注自蒸馏的以数据为中心的框架，它利用内部神经元激活来指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建。然后，通过从教师分布进行在线策略蒸馏来训练模型，在任何阶段都不需要真实标签。在专业领域基准测试上，Neuron-OPSD在提升领域内任务性能的同时，保持了跨领域泛化能力，并缓解了先前无标注基线方法的校准误差膨胀问题。该框架尤其适用于在线交互或外部监督成本高昂或不可行的场景，并且在概念上区别于依赖已标注奖励轨迹的离线RL方法。

</details>


### [243] [Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02499)
*Gil Harari,Yoel Zimmermann,Ola Tangen Kulseng,Laura Zichi,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了矩阵结构化优化器（Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon）与Adam在训练机器学习原子间势（MLIP）模型（NequIP和Allegro）时的性能，发现SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于Adam，尤其在部分力监督下提升更为明显，表明优化器选择是MLIP训练中被忽视但影响重大的设计维度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MLIP已成为AI科学模拟的标志性方法，但训练优化器的选择长期以来被忽视，社区默认使用Adam及其变体。本文旨在探索和系统比较新型矩阵结构化优化器对MLIP训练效果的影响。

Method: 实现并系统比较了一类近期提出的矩阵结构化优化器——Muon、SOAP以及混合的SOAP-Muon，用于训练NequIP和Allegro两种MLIP模型，并与Adam优化器进行基准对比。

Result: SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上均显著优于Adam，且表现稳健；Muon仅相对Adam有部分提升；在部分力监督设置下，改进幅度尤为突出。

Conclusion: 优化器选择是MLIP训练中一个被忽视但极具影响力的设计维度，SOAP和SOAP-Muon是训练MLIP模型的更优选择。

Original Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习原子间势（MLIP）已成为人工智能科学模拟的标志性方法。尽管在新架构和数据集上的努力催生了越来越准确和通用的模型，但训练优化器的选择在很大程度上仍未得到探索，社区默认使用Adam及其变体。在本文中，我们实现并系统比较了一类近期提出的矩阵结构化优化器——包括Muon、SOAP以及混合的SOAP-Muon——用于训练NequIP和Allegro MLIP模型。我们发现，这些优化器在收敛速度和最终精度上均可显著优于Adam。SOAP和SOAP-Muon展现出稳健且持续优异的表现，而Muon相较于Adam仅提供了部分增益。在部分力监督设置下，改进尤为明显。我们的结果表明，优化器选择是MLIP训练中一个被忽视但影响重大的设计维度。

</details>


### [244] [DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Wenhao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DemoPSD框架，通过对教师分布与学生分布进行自适应反向KL重心融合，解决on-policy自蒸馏中教师密集token级监督导致的特权信息泄露和探索能力抑制问题，在科学推理任务上优于GRPO和SDPO。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: On-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）中，教师基于特权信息（privileged information）提供密集token级监督，导致两个关键问题：(1) 特权信息泄露：学生编码了测试时不可用的答案相关捷径；(2) 探索抑制：过度拟合领域内模式，损害跨域泛化能力。

Method: 提出DemoPSD框架，核心思想是选择性采纳教师指导。具体做法是：不直接拟合完整教师分布，而是将学生导向一个反向KL重心目标（reverse-KL barycenter target）——即教师分布与学生分布的加权几何组合。通过衡量两分布之间的差异，自适应地在每个token位置控制混合权重，从而在向教师学习和保持自身推理能力之间取得自然平衡。

Result: (1) 理论证明：DemoPSD实现了泄露衰减（有效缓解特权信息泄露）和探索保留（在密集token级蒸馏下保留探索能力）；(2) 实验验证：在SciKnowEval的四个科学领域上优于GRPO和SDPO，同时保持更高的训练熵，并在OOD的GPQA基准上展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DemoPSD通过自适应反向KL重心融合，有效解决了OPSD中特权信息泄露和探索抑制两个根本性缺陷，提升了LLM推理训练的泛化性和探索能力。

Original Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 在策略自蒸馏（OPSD）已成为训练大型语言模型（LLM）进行推理的一种实用方法，其中单个模型同时扮演教师和学生的角色，但两者拥有不同级别的信息访问权限。然而，近期研究发现，教师基于特权信息提供的密集token级监督会导致对领域内模式的过拟合、抑制探索、损害跨域泛化，同时引入一个更根本的问题：*特权信息泄露*——学生编码了测试时不可用的答案依赖捷径。我们提出**DemoPSD**，一个通过*选择性采纳教师指导*思想解决上述问题的新框架。DemoPSD不直接拟合完整的教师分布，而是将学生导向一个*反向KL重心目标*——教师分布与学生分布的加权几何组合——该目标自然地平衡了向教师学习与保留学生自身推理能力之间的权衡。我们衡量两分布之间的差异，并利用这种差异自适应地控制每个token位置的混合程度。我们可证明地展示了DemoPSD实现了**(1)** *泄露衰减*，即有效缓解特权信息泄露；以及**(2)** *探索保留*，即在密集token级蒸馏下保留探索能力。在SciKnowEval四个科学领域的广泛实验表明，DemoPSD在保持更高训练熵的同时优于GRPO和SDPO，并在分布外（OOD）的GPQA基准上展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

</details>


### [245] [Program-as-Weights: A Programming Paradigm for Fuzzy Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02512)
*Wentao Zhang,Liliana Hotsko,Woojeong Kim,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出模糊函数编程范式，将自然语言规范编译为轻量本地可执行神经工件；PAW方法用4B编译器生成适配器，0.6B解释器执行效果媲美32B直接提示，推理内存仅约1/50。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 日常编程任务（如日志告警、JSON修复、搜索结果排序）难以用规则实现，日益依赖LLM API，导致本地性、可复现性和成本问题，需一种低成本、本地可执行且可复用的替代方案。

Method: 提出Program-as-Weights (PAW)：基于FuzzyBench（1000万示例数据集）训练4B编译器，将自然语言规范编译为参数高效适配器，由冻结的轻量解释器（0.6B Qwen3）执行。将基础模型从逐输入的问题求解器转变为工具构建器。

Result: 0.6B Qwen3解释器执行PAW程序在性能上匹配Qwen3-32B直接提示，推理内存仅约1/50，在MacBook M3上达到30 tokens/s。

Conclusion: PAW范式将大模型从每次调用都需推理的在线求解器，转变为一次定义、多次廉价离线复用的工具构建器，在保持性能的同时大幅降低推理成本和提升本地性。

Original Abstract: Many everyday programming tasks resist clean rule-based implementation, such as alerting on important log lines, repairing malformed JSON, or ranking search results by intent, and are increasingly outsourced to large language model APIs at the cost of locality, reproducibility, and price. We propose fuzzy-function programming: compiling such a function from a natural-language specification into a compact, locally-executable neural artifact. We instantiate this paradigm with Program-as-Weights (PAW), in which a 4B compiler trained on FuzzyBench, a 10M-example dataset we release, emits parameter-efficient adapters for a frozen, lightweight interpreter. A 0.6B Qwen3 interpreter executing PAW programs matches the performance of direct prompting of Qwen3-32B, while using roughly one fiftieth of the inference memory and running at 30 tokens/s on a MacBook M3. PAW reframes the foundation model from a per-input problem solver into a tool builder: invoked once per function definition, it produces a small reusable artifact whose subsequent calls per function application are cheap and offline.

中文摘要翻译: 许多日常编程任务（如对重要日志行进行告警、修复畸形JSON或按意图对搜索结果进行排序）难以通过干净的基于规则的方式实现，因此越来越多地被外包给大语言模型API，但代价是牺牲了本地性、可复现性和成本。我们提出模糊函数编程（fuzzy-function programming）：将此类函数从自然语言规范编译成一个紧凑的、可本地执行的神经工件。我们通过Program-as-Weights (PAW)实例化该范式：一个在FuzzyBench（我们发布的含1000万示例的数据集）上训练的4B编译器，为冻结的轻量级解释器生成参数高效适配器。执行PAW程序的0.6B Qwen3解释器在性能上与直接提示Qwen3-32B相当，而推理内存仅为其约五十分之一，并在MacBook M3上以30 tokens/s的速度运行。PAW将基础模型从逐输入的问题求解器重新定义为工具构建器：每次函数定义调用一次，生成一个小的可复用工件，其后续每次函数应用的调用都是廉价且离线的。

</details>
